Progress-sync integrity repair

Ninth amendment pass. This is the source-grounded implementation contract for one document-wide owner coordinator, valid no-op hydration for unmounted apps, lossless client lineage, explicit guest adoption, locked server saves, a typed metadata policy, and mandatory real-database proof.

Statusimplementation-ready Branchfix/progress-sync-integrity Artifact2026-08-03-progress-sync-integrity.html Passninth amendment Edit scopethis HTML plan only

Contents

  1. Evidence and round-seven closure
  2. Owner tuple, state machine, and first-sync truth table
  3. Singleton store binding, hydration, converters, and rollback
  4. Foreign ownership, purge, guest transfer, and mutation provenance
  5. Attempt lineage, adoption, and client wire builders
  6. Typed server wire, route factory, and Drizzle transaction
  7. Isolated PostgreSQL, faults, and Vitest isolation
  8. SyncMetadataV1 and recursive policy grammar
  9. Source-grounded 32-schema semantic matrix
  10. One authoritative input graph and exact inventory
  11. Allowed implementation files and TDD order
  12. Acceptance IDs and deterministic verification

1. Evidence and round-seven closure

This pass was checked against graphify-out/graph.json using the graphify fast-path query, the full current plan, all three fresh round-seven gate reports, installed Zustand 5.0.14 declarations and runtime, Drizzle ORM 0.45.2, Node pg 8.22.0, Vitest 4.1.10, PostgreSQL 17.9 tools, package manifests, current source stores and callers, partializers, schemas, route, auth construction, database schema, tests, and recent git history. The verified branch is fix/progress-sync-integrity at 7649017. Existing untracked docs/ and graphify-out/ work is preserved. This amendment changes no product code, tests, configuration, lockfile, graphify artifact, lesson, or instruction file.

The proof names in this closure table are stable case IDs, not filesystem paths. The generated proof registry maps each case ID to one exact inventoried file and one exact test name. The registry validator requires bidirectional equality between closure IDs and registry IDs, rejects duplicate IDs or test names, rejects duplicate file/test pairs, and rejects a registry path outside Section 11. This keeps the gate table readable while preserving a mechanically executable implementation boundary.

1All 32 registrations are preallocated as branded virtual-noop hydration slots, accepted by READY, and replaced only by guarded live bindings after rehydrate.
2SynchronizedStoreApi is the callable Zustand 5 UseBoundStore hybrid with a local readonly StoreApi pick, typed setState, and the installed declaration probe.
3useAuthSync() and the generic compatibility overload use stable external-store subscription, argumentless owner access, explicit cleanup, and owner-rotation teardown.
4Hill Climb, Monster Truck, and Oregon persist lastModified:0, migrate version-0 records, write the injected clock only at completion, and prove getter purity.
5The historical union and one PersistMigrationRecipe declare v0/missing, Checkers v2, and Oregon v1 decoding and migration before envelope validation.
6Marker loss starts a server recovery probe, uses the full tuple and durable replay fields, and cannot treat marker B or a client epoch as authority.S5
7Purge enumerates payloads, then ledger, staging, sign-out, app targets, and Chess with old-owner validation, marker B, read-back, quarantine, and typed cleanup authorization.
8The transaction algorithm explicitly acquires the user lock, verifies authority, acquires the app lock, then performs the GET read; the lock observer and required GET case inspect the real sequence.S3
9Direct setProgress, Breakout useBreakoutStore.setState, store actions, and adoption callbacks are discriminated source partitions with source ranges and fingerprints.
10Every persisted API calls getOptions() through Partial<PersistOptions> narrowing, with no unsafe assumption about Zustand's partial return.
11Required integration cases carry branded invocation tokens, non-empty response/row/lock/envelope/DML evidence, receipt-count enforcement, and actual isolated route execution.S8
12HomeClient consumes subscribed useOwnerSnapshot; the inventory names that hook boundary and forbids direct marker/local access.
13Quarantine records and indexes use only recovery-cleanup after completed live reset; foreign purge cannot authorize them.
14Metadata capacity is independently derived, rendered at 32768 entries and 4194304 bytes, and witnessed by A10 fanout 5000 plus A25 depth 3.
15Every persisted path is rooted, encoded, identity-scoped, and tied to an explicit source identity field or concrete path.
16A25 expands saved-beat, dynamic sound key, and dense index scopes in order, with invalid nested wildcard forms rejected by the grammar.
17Presence rules are mandatory AST/source facts, distinguish missing, null, composite null, and default, and are enforced before traversal and metadata allocation.
18A26 petName is sourced under settings with its own LWW binding and source-oracle fact, not inferred from a mismatched matrix path.
19Mutation provenance covers user actions, direct callers, direct API setters, adoption callbacks, async completion, and idempotent source getters.
20The proof runner uses private contract and execution-registry brands, function hooks, literal Vitest cases, actual result lifecycle events, and exact two-config coverage.S8
21Beacon transport is selected from raw query/header occurrences before auth or quota, with duplicate preservation and deterministic mismatch correlation.S1
22The browser performs structural owner-lease checks only; HMAC signing and verification remain server-only behind the secret boundary.S2
23progress_owner_epochs has an explicit user FK cascade, and the typed user deletion wrapper locks, deletes, verifies zero authority rows before commit, and is wired from auth.S4
24Claim and recovery nonces, requested tuples, replay leases, and nonce-reuse errors are durable and idempotent under the user lock.S5
25The stored-owner fixture expects exact 409 owner_mismatch with unchanged rows and timestamps, not a generic stored conflict.S6
26First save is merge:false with baseServerVersion:null; real concurrent absent-row setup proves one 200 winner, one 409 server_changed loser, and post-lock readback.S7
27Projection wrappers, source extractors, validator, DML executor, integration receipts, proof contracts, reporter lifecycle, and generated/source projections are all function-valued and mechanically rejected when absent or late.S8

The server review labels S1 through S8 are evidence cross-references only. They add no proof IDs and no files. The existing 21 required IDs, 162-file inventory, 32 A-cells, closure projection, rendered projection, and generated/source cardinalities remain unchanged.

type ProofCaseBinding = {
  proofId: string;
  gate: "client" | "server" | "policy";
  file: string;
  testName: string;
};
declare const proofCaseContractBrand: unique symbol;
declare const proofObservationBrand: unique symbol;
declare const proofExecutionRegistryBrand: unique symbol;
type ProofObservation = {
  readonly [proofObservationBrand]: true;
  proofId: string;
  phase: "setup" | "assertion" | "terminal";
  source: "runtime" | "source-oracle" | "wire" | "database";
  digest: string;
};
type ProofObservationLedger = {
  recordSetup(observation: ProofObservation): void;
  recordAssertion(observation: ProofObservation): void;
  recordTerminal(observation: ProofObservation): void;
  requireComplete(proofId: string): NonEmptyReadonly<ProofObservation>;
};
type ProofCaseExecutionContext = {
  proofId: string;
  runner: "ordinary" | "integration";
  observations: ProofObservationLedger;
  executionRegistry: ProofExecutionRegistry;
  actualTestInvocation: () => void;
};
type ProofCaseTerminalObservation = {
  readonly [proofObservationBrand]: true;
  proofId: string;
  oracleInvocationToken: RequiredIntegrationInvocationToken | null;
  projectorInvocation: ProjectionInvocationEvidence | null;
  terminalObservation: ProofObservation;
};
function createProofTerminalObservation(
  proofId: string,
  evidence: IntegrationTerminalEvidence,
  terminalObservation: ProofObservation
): ProofCaseTerminalObservation {
  if (evidence[integrationTerminalEvidenceBrand] !== true || evidence.id !== proofId || evidence.oracleInvocationToken === null || terminalObservation[proofObservationBrand] !== true || terminalObservation.proofId !== proofId || terminalObservation.phase !== "terminal") throw new Error("integration evidence cannot become proof terminal evidence");
  return {
    [proofObservationBrand]: true,
    proofId,
    oracleInvocationToken: evidence.oracleInvocationToken,
    projectorInvocation: evidence.projectionInvocation,
    terminalObservation
  };
}
type ProofCaseSeed = ProofCaseBinding & {
  setup(context: ProofCaseExecutionContext): Promise<void>;
  assertions(context: ProofCaseExecutionContext): Promise<void>;
  terminalEvidence(context: ProofCaseExecutionContext): Promise<ProofCaseTerminalObservation>;
};
type ProofCaseExecutionEvidence = {
  readonly [proofObservationBrand]: true;
  proofId: string;
  runner: "ordinary" | "integration";
  terminal: "passed";
  testInvoked: true;
  setupCalls: 1;
  assertionCalls: 1;
  terminalEvidenceCalls: 1;
  oracleInvocationToken: RequiredIntegrationInvocationToken | null;
  projectorInvocation: ProjectionInvocationEvidence | null;
  observations: NonEmptyReadonly<ProofObservation>;
};
type ProofExecutionRegistry = {
  readonly [proofExecutionRegistryBrand]: true;
  record(evidence: ProofCaseExecutionEvidence): void;
  get(proofId: string): ProofCaseExecutionEvidence | undefined;
  ids(): readonly string[];
};
function createProofExecutionRegistry(expectedIds: readonly string[]): ProofExecutionRegistry {
  const expected = new Set(expectedIds);
  const executions = new Map<string, ProofCaseExecutionEvidence>();
  return {
    [proofExecutionRegistryBrand]: true,
    record(evidence) {
      if (evidence[proofObservationBrand] !== true || !expected.has(evidence.proofId) || executions.has(evidence.proofId)) throw new Error("proof execution registry rejected evidence");
      executions.set(evidence.proofId, evidence);
    },
    get: proofId => executions.get(proofId),
    ids: () => [...executions.keys()].sort()
  };
}
type ProofCaseContract = {
  readonly [proofCaseContractBrand]: true;
  readonly binding: ProofCaseBinding;
  readonly setup: ProofCaseSeed["setup"];
  readonly assertions: ProofCaseSeed["assertions"];
  readonly terminalEvidence: ProofCaseSeed["terminalEvidence"];
  run(context: ProofCaseExecutionContext): Promise<ProofCaseExecutionEvidence>;
};

  | "progress.error.stored-conflict-no-dml"
  | "progress.error.invalid-projection-no-dml"

declare const requiredIntegrationOracleBrand: unique symbol;
declare const integrationTerminalEvidenceBrand: unique symbol;
declare const requiredIntegrationInvocationBrand: unique symbol;
type NonEmptyReadonly<T> = readonly [T, ...T[]];
type RequiredIntegrationInvocationToken = string & { readonly [requiredIntegrationInvocationBrand]: true };
declare function createRequiredIntegrationInvocationToken(id: RequiredIntegrationId): RequiredIntegrationInvocationToken;
type RequiredIntegrationEvidenceKind = "response-and-row-snapshot" | "response-and-lock-observation" | "response-and-envelope-roundtrip";
type IntegrationDmlEvent = {
  sequence: number;
  routeInvocationId: string;
  transactionId: string;
  table: string;
  operation: "begin" | "advisory-lock" | "select" | "insert" | "update" | "delete" | "commit" | "rollback";
  dml: boolean;
};
type IntegrationLockEvent = {
  sequence: number;
  routeInvocationId: string;
  transactionId: string;
  kind: "user-lock-request" | "user-lock-grant" | "authority-for-update" | "app-lock-request" | "app-lock-grant" | "app-select-after-lock";
  backendPid: number;
};
declare const getLockOrderEvidenceBrand: unique symbol;
type GetLockOrderEvidence =
  | {
      readonly [getLockOrderEvidenceBrand]: true;
      mode: "get";
      routeInvocationId: string;
      transactionId: string;
      userLockGrantSequence: number;
      authorityForUpdateSequence: number;
      appLockGrantSequence: number;
      appSelectSequence: number;
      recoveryAppLockCount: 0;
    }
  | {
      readonly [getLockOrderEvidenceBrand]: true;
      mode: "authority-probe";
      routeInvocationId: string;
      transactionId: string;
      userLockGrantSequence: number;
      authorityForUpdateSequence: number;
      appLockRequestCount: 0;
      appLockGrantCount: 0;
      appSelectCount: 0;
      recoveryAppLockCount: 0;
    };
type GetReadLockOrderEvidence = Extract<GetLockOrderEvidence, { mode: "get" }>;
type AuthorityProbeLockEvidence = Extract<GetLockOrderEvidence, { mode: "authority-probe" }>;
type ProgressTableName = "app_progress" | "gaming_profiles" | "leaderboard_entries" | "progress_owner_epochs";
type ExactTableCountEvidence = {
  table: ProgressTableName;
  beforeCount: number;
  afterCount: number;
  beforeDigest: string;
  afterDigest: string;
};
type IntegrationDmlSummary = {
  total: number;
  byTable: Readonly<Record<ProgressTableName, number>>;
  digest: string;
};
type ExactTableDmlExpectation = {
  counts: Readonly<Record<ProgressTableName, { before: number; after: number }>>;
  dmlByTable: Readonly<Record<ProgressTableName, number>>;
  dmlTotal: number;
};
type IntegrationRowSnapshot = {
  capturedAtSequence: number;
  tables: NonEmptyReadonly<{ table: string; rowCount: number; digest: string }>;
  complete: true;
};
type IntegrationResponseEvidence = {
  routeInvocationId: string;
  status: number;
  body: unknown;
  bodyDigest: string;
  correlation: CorrelationV1;
};
type IntegrationResponseExpectation = {
  status: number;
  success: boolean;
  errorCode: ProgressErrorCode | null;
  responseKind: "post-success" | "error";
};
type IntegrationTerminalEvidence = {
  id: RequiredIntegrationId;
  kind: RequiredIntegrationEvidenceKind;
  readonly [integrationTerminalEvidenceBrand]: true;
  completionDigest: string;
  response: IntegrationResponseEvidence;
  rowSnapshot: IntegrationRowSnapshot;
  lockObservation: readonly IntegrationLockEvent[];

  dmlEvents: readonly IntegrationDmlEvent[];
  tableCounts: readonly [ExactTableCountEvidence, ExactTableCountEvidence, ExactTableCountEvidence, ExactTableCountEvidence];
  dmlSummary: IntegrationDmlSummary;
  transactionObserved: boolean;
  projectionInvocation: ProjectionInvocationEvidence;
  lockOrder: GetReadLockOrderEvidence | null;
  authorityProbeLock: AuthorityProbeLockEvidence | null;
  setupObservationCount: number;
  assertionObservationCount: number;
  oracleInvocationToken: RequiredIntegrationInvocationToken;
};

type ConcurrentFirstSaveSetupReceipt = Extract<IntegrationObservationReceipt, { phase: "setup" }> & {
  scenario: "progress.first-save.concurrent-absent";
  firstSaveRoles: ConcurrentFirstSaveObservedRoles;
};
type ConcurrentFirstSaveAssertionReceipt = Extract<IntegrationObservationReceipt, { phase: "assertion" }> & {
  scenario: "progress.first-save.concurrent-absent";
  firstSaveRoles: ConcurrentFirstSaveObservedRoles;
};
declare const integrationScenarioCompletionBrand: unique symbol;
type IntegrationScenarioCompletion = {
  readonly [integrationScenarioCompletionBrand]: true;
  scenario: RequiredIntegrationId;
  setup: Extract<IntegrationObservationReceipt, { phase: "setup" }>;
  assertion: Extract<IntegrationObservationReceipt, { phase: "assertion" }>;
  observedRouteInvocationIds: NonEmptyReadonly<string>;
  digest: string;
};
type IntegrationEvidenceLedger = {
  seedConcurrentFirstSave(options: ConcurrentFirstSaveSetup): Promise<ConcurrentFirstSaveSetupReceipt>;
  getConcurrentFirstSaveSetup(): ConcurrentFirstSaveSetupReceipt;
  assertConcurrentWinnerLoser(options: ConcurrentFirstSaveAssertion): Promise<ConcurrentFirstSaveAssertionReceipt>;
  seedGetAfterAppLock(options: GetAfterAppLockSetup): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  assertGetWaitedThenRead(options: GetAfterAppLockAssertion): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  seedConcurrentPostDelete(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  assertLinearizedOrder(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  seedTimestampPost(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  assertTimestampsAfterLock(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  holdUserAndAppLocks(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  installStatementProbe(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  assertSqlState(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  seedMalformedRequestAndSnapshots(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  seedForeignOwnerEnvelopeAndSnapshots(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  seedValidCanonicalWithRealExtractorCorruption(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  installPostDmlFaultAndSnapshots(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  seedMissingDelete(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  assertNoDmlError(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  assertExactTableDml(expected: ExactTableDmlExpectation): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  seedCookieArrays(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  seedPositiveProjectionWithEightHandleCollisions(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  seedValidNoScore(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  seedEnvelopeCase(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  assertEnvelopeCase(options: IntegrationScenarioSpec): Promise<IntegrationObservationReceipt>;
  terminalEvidence(kind: RequiredIntegrationEvidenceKind, token: RequiredIntegrationInvocationToken, completion: IntegrationScenarioCompletion): Promise<IntegrationTerminalEvidence>;
  assertScenarioLedgerComplete(id: RequiredIntegrationId): IntegrationScenarioCompletion;
};
function assertObservedFirstSaveRoleBinding(
  receipt: ConcurrentFirstSaveSetupReceipt,
  bindObservedRoles: ConcurrentFirstSaveRoleBinding
): { winnerRequestId: "request-a" | "request-b"; loserRequestId: "request-a" | "request-b" } {
  if (receipt[integrationObservationReceiptBrand] !== true || receipt.phase !== "setup" || receipt.scenario !== "progress.first-save.concurrent-absent") throw new Error("first-save setup receipt is not a runtime observation");
  const requestIds = new Set(receipt.requestIds);
  const observed = receipt.firstSaveRoles;
  const bound = bindObservedRoles(receipt);
  if (requestIds.size !== 2 || !requestIds.has("request-a") || !requestIds.has("request-b") || bound.winnerRequestId === bound.loserRequestId || bound.winnerRequestId !== observed.winnerRequestId || bound.loserRequestId !== observed.loserRequestId || observed.firstGrant !== observed.winnerRequestId || observed.bothWaitersObserved !== true || observed.winnerObservedAbsent !== true || observed.loserQueuedOnUserLock !== true) throw new Error("first-save role binding was not derived from the observed setup receipt");
  return bound;
}
function deriveGetLockOrderEvidence(
  events: NonEmptyReadonly<IntegrationLockEvent>,
  routeInvocationId: string,
  mode: "get" | "recover" | "claim"
): GetLockOrderEvidence {
  if (events.some(event => event.routeInvocationId !== routeInvocationId)) throw new Error("lock event belongs to another route invocation");
  const transactionIds = new Set(events.map(event => event.transactionId));
  if (transactionIds.size !== 1) throw new Error("lock evidence spans multiple transactions");
  const transactionId = events[0].transactionId;
  const userGrant = events.find(event => event.kind === "user-lock-grant");
  const authority = events.find(event => event.kind === "authority-for-update");
  if (userGrant === undefined || authority === undefined || events.filter(event => event.kind === "user-lock-grant").length !== 1 || events.filter(event => event.kind === "authority-for-update").length !== 1 || userGrant.sequence >= authority.sequence) throw new Error("authority lock trace is incomplete or out of order");
  if (mode !== "get") {
    if (events.some(event => event.kind === "app-lock-request" || event.kind === "app-lock-grant" || event.kind === "app-select-after-lock")) throw new Error("authority probe acquired an app lock");
    return { [getLockOrderEvidenceBrand]: true, mode: "authority-probe", routeInvocationId, transactionId, userLockGrantSequence: userGrant.sequence, authorityForUpdateSequence: authority.sequence, appLockRequestCount: 0, appLockGrantCount: 0, appSelectCount: 0, recoveryAppLockCount: 0 };
  }
  const appRequest = events.find(event => event.kind === "app-lock-request");
  const appGrant = events.find(event => event.kind === "app-lock-grant");
  const appSelect = events.find(event => event.kind === "app-select-after-lock");
  if (appRequest === undefined || appGrant === undefined || appSelect === undefined || events.filter(event => event.kind === "app-lock-request").length !== 1 || events.filter(event => event.kind === "app-lock-grant").length !== 1 || events.filter(event => event.kind === "app-select-after-lock").length !== 1 || authority.sequence >= appRequest.sequence || appRequest.sequence >= appGrant.sequence || appGrant.sequence >= appSelect.sequence) throw new Error("GET lock trace is incomplete or out of order");
  return { [getLockOrderEvidenceBrand]: true, mode: "get", routeInvocationId, transactionId, userLockGrantSequence: userGrant.sequence, authorityForUpdateSequence: authority.sequence, appLockGrantSequence: appGrant.sequence, appSelectSequence: appSelect.sequence, recoveryAppLockCount: 0 };
}
function deriveIntegrationDmlSummary(events: readonly IntegrationDmlEvent[], routeInvocationId: string): IntegrationDmlSummary {
  const byTable: Record<ProgressTableName, number> = { app_progress: 0, gaming_profiles: 0, leaderboard_entries: 0, progress_owner_epochs: 0 };
  if (events.some(event => event.routeInvocationId !== routeInvocationId)) throw new Error("DML event belongs to another route invocation");
  for (const event of events) {
    if (!event.dml) continue;
    if (!(event.table in byTable)) throw new Error("DML event is not bound to an inventoried progress table");
    byTable[event.table as ProgressTableName] += 1;
  }
  const total = Object.values(byTable).reduce((sum, count) => sum + count, 0);
  return { total, byTable, digest: sha256(stableJson({ routeInvocationId, total, byTable })) };
}
function assertExactTableDmlObservation(
  tableCounts: readonly [ExactTableCountEvidence, ExactTableCountEvidence, ExactTableCountEvidence, ExactTableCountEvidence],
  dmlEvents: readonly IntegrationDmlEvent[],
  dmlSummary: IntegrationDmlSummary,
  routeInvocationId: string,
  expected: ExactTableDmlExpectation
): void {
  const tableNames = tableCounts.map(snapshot => snapshot.table);
  if (tableNames.length !== 4 || new Set(tableNames).size !== 4 || tableNames.some(table => !(table in expected.counts))) throw new Error("exact table evidence is incomplete");
  const derived = deriveIntegrationDmlSummary(dmlEvents, routeInvocationId);
  if (derived.digest !== dmlSummary.digest || derived.total !== dmlSummary.total || stableJson(derived.byTable) !== stableJson(dmlSummary.byTable) || derived.total !== expected.dmlTotal) throw new Error("DML summary is not derived from route events");
  for (const snapshot of tableCounts) {
    const expectedTable = expected.counts[snapshot.table];
    if (expectedTable === undefined || snapshot.beforeCount !== expectedTable.before || snapshot.afterCount !== expectedTable.after || snapshot.beforeDigest.length === 0 || snapshot.afterDigest.length === 0 || dmlSummary.byTable[snapshot.table] !== expected.dmlByTable[snapshot.table]) throw new Error("exact table count or DML expectation failed");
  }
  if (Object.values(expected.dmlByTable).reduce((sum, count) => sum + count, 0) !== expected.dmlTotal) throw new Error("exact DML expectation total is inconsistent");
}
function assertExactTableDmlEvidence(evidence: IntegrationTerminalEvidence, expected: ExactTableDmlExpectation): void {
  assertExactTableDmlObservation(evidence.tableCounts, evidence.dmlEvents, evidence.dmlSummary, evidence.response.routeInvocationId, expected);
}
type IntegrationBarrierSet = {
  awaitLockObservation(): Promise<void>;
  release(): Promise<void>;
};
type RequiredIntegrationContext = {
  id: RequiredIntegrationId;
  invocationToken: RequiredIntegrationInvocationToken;
  fixture: ProgressSyncFixtureBuilder;
  route: ProgressRequestContextFactory;
  db: ProgressDbHandle;
  barriers: IntegrationBarrierSet;
  evidence: IntegrationEvidenceLedger;
  calls: { setup: number; assertions: number; terminalEvidence: number };
};
type RequiredIntegrationOracle = {
  readonly [requiredIntegrationOracleBrand]: true;
  readonly id: RequiredIntegrationId;
  readonly invocationToken: RequiredIntegrationInvocationToken;
  readonly terminalEvidenceKind: RequiredIntegrationEvidenceKind;
  setup(context: RequiredIntegrationContext): Promise<void>;
  assertions(context: RequiredIntegrationContext): Promise<void>;
  terminalEvidence(context: RequiredIntegrationContext): Promise<IntegrationTerminalEvidence>;
};
function createRequiredIntegrationOracle(
  id: RequiredIntegrationId,
  terminalEvidenceKind: RequiredIntegrationEvidenceKind,
  setup: (context: RequiredIntegrationContext) => Promise<void>,
  assertions: (context: RequiredIntegrationContext) => Promise<void>
): RequiredIntegrationOracle {
  const invocationToken = createRequiredIntegrationInvocationToken(id);
  return {
    [requiredIntegrationOracleBrand]: true,
    id,
    invocationToken,
    terminalEvidenceKind,
    async setup(context) {
      context.calls.setup += 1;
      await setup(context);
    },
    async assertions(context) {
      context.calls.assertions += 1;
      await assertions(context);
    },
    async terminalEvidence(context) {
      context.calls.terminalEvidence += 1;
      const completion = context.evidence.assertScenarioLedgerComplete(context.id);
      const evidence = await context.evidence.terminalEvidence(terminalEvidenceKind, invocationToken, completion);
      if (evidence.id !== id || evidence.kind !== terminalEvidenceKind || evidence.oracleInvocationToken !== invocationToken || evidence[integrationTerminalEvidenceBrand] !== true) throw new Error("required integration terminal evidence mismatch");
      if (evidence.completionDigest !== completion.digest) throw new Error("required integration completion was not bound to terminal evidence");
      return evidence;
    }
  };
}

function assertRequiredIntegrationCaseRegistry(
  expectedIds: readonly string[],
  cases: readonly RequiredIntegrationCaseBinding[],
  fileInventory: readonly FileInventoryInput[]
): void {
  const expected = [...expectedIds].sort();
  const actual = cases.map((caseBinding) => caseBinding.id).sort();
  if (expected.length !== 21 || new Set(expected).size !== 21 || expected.join("\n") !== actual.join("\n")) throw new Error("required integration case set mismatch");
  const files = new Set(fileInventory.map((row) => row.path));
  for (const caseBinding of cases) {
    const oracle = REQUIRED_INTEGRATION_ORACLES[caseBinding.id];
    if (caseBinding.testName !== caseBinding.id || !files.has(caseBinding.file) || !caseBinding.file.endsWith(".integration.test.ts") || caseBinding.oracle !== oracle || oracle.id !== caseBinding.id || oracle[requiredIntegrationOracleBrand] !== true || oracle.invocationToken === null || typeof oracle.setup !== "function" || typeof oracle.assertions !== "function" || typeof oracle.terminalEvidence !== "function") throw new Error("required integration case binding or oracle mismatch");
  }
}
async function runRequiredIntegrationCase(
  binding: RequiredIntegrationCaseBinding,
  context: RequiredIntegrationContext
): Promise<IntegrationTerminalEvidence> {
  const before = { ...context.calls };
  if (context.invocationToken !== binding.oracle.invocationToken) throw new Error("required integration context token mismatch");
  const evidence = await binding.oracle.setup(context).then(() => binding.oracle.assertions(context)).then(() => binding.oracle.terminalEvidence(context));
  const completion = context.evidence.assertScenarioLedgerComplete(context.id);
  assertRequiredIntegrationInvocation(context, evidence, completion);
  if (context.calls.setup !== before.setup + 1 || context.calls.assertions !== before.assertions + 1 || context.calls.terminalEvidence !== before.terminalEvidence + 1) throw new Error("required integration oracle call count mismatch");
  return evidence;
}
function assertRequiredIntegrationInvocation(
  context: RequiredIntegrationContext,
  evidence: IntegrationTerminalEvidence,
  completion: IntegrationScenarioCompletion
): void {
  if (context.calls.setup !== 1 || context.calls.assertions !== 1 || context.calls.terminalEvidence !== 1) throw new Error("required integration oracle was not invoked exactly once");
  if (evidence.id !== context.id || evidence.oracleInvocationToken !== context.invocationToken || evidence[integrationTerminalEvidenceBrand] !== true) throw new Error("required integration oracle invocation token missing");
  if (completion[integrationScenarioCompletionBrand] !== true || completion.scenario !== context.id || completion.setup.phase !== "setup" || completion.assertion.phase !== "assertion" || completion.observedRouteInvocationIds.length === 0 || completion.digest !== evidence.completionDigest || !completion.observedRouteInvocationIds.includes(evidence.response.routeInvocationId)) throw new Error("required integration evidence completion is missing or disconnected");
  const traceObserved = evidence.dmlEvents.length > 0 || evidence.lockObservation.length > 0;
  const derivedDml = deriveIntegrationDmlSummary(evidence.dmlEvents, evidence.response.routeInvocationId);
  const tableNames = evidence.tableCounts.map(snapshot => snapshot.table);
  if (evidence.transactionObserved !== traceObserved || evidence.response.routeInvocationId.length === 0 || evidence.rowSnapshot.complete !== true || evidence.projectionInvocation[projectionInvocationEvidenceBrand] !== true || evidence.projectionInvocation.combined !== true || evidence.setupObservationCount !== 1 || evidence.assertionObservationCount !== 1 || tableNames.length !== 4 || new Set(tableNames).size !== 4 || derivedDml.digest !== evidence.dmlSummary.digest || derivedDml.total !== evidence.dmlSummary.total || stableJson(derivedDml.byTable) !== stableJson(evidence.dmlSummary.byTable)) throw new Error("required integration terminal evidence is empty or was not observed");
  if (evidence.transactionObserved && evidence.lockObservation.length === 0) throw new Error("transactional integration evidence has no lock trace");
  if (evidence.authorityProbeLock !== null && (evidence.authorityProbeLock[getLockOrderEvidenceBrand] !== true || evidence.authorityProbeLock.mode !== "authority-probe" || evidence.authorityProbeLock.routeInvocationId.length === 0 || evidence.authorityProbeLock.appLockRequestCount !== 0 || evidence.authorityProbeLock.appLockGrantCount !== 0 || evidence.authorityProbeLock.appSelectCount !== 0 || evidence.authorityProbeLock.recoveryAppLockCount !== 0)) throw new Error("authority probe lock evidence is fabricated or acquired an app lock");
  if (context.id === "progress.lock.read-after-lock" && (evidence.lockOrder === null || evidence.lockOrder[getLockOrderEvidenceBrand] !== true || evidence.lockOrder.mode !== "get" || evidence.lockOrder.routeInvocationId !== evidence.response.routeInvocationId || evidence.lockOrder.recoveryAppLockCount !== 0 || evidence.lockOrder.userLockGrantSequence >= evidence.lockOrder.authorityForUpdateSequence || evidence.lockOrder.authorityForUpdateSequence >= evidence.lockOrder.appLockGrantSequence || evidence.lockOrder.appLockGrantSequence >= evidence.lockOrder.appSelectSequence || evidence.authorityProbeLock !== null)) throw new Error("GET lock order evidence is incomplete or out of order");
  if (evidence.kind === "response-and-envelope-roundtrip" && evidence.envelopeRoundTrip.length === 0) throw new Error("envelope integration evidence has no round-trip trace");
  if (!evidence.transactionObserved && (evidence.envelopeRoundTrip.length !== 0 || evidence.projectionInvocation.firstDmlSequence !== null || evidence.projectionInvocation.projectorCalls !== 0 || evidence.projectionInvocation.validatorCalls !== 0 || evidence.projectionInvocation.realExtractorCalled || evidence.lockOrder !== null)) throw new Error("preflight no-DML evidence contains a fabricated transaction or projection");
  if (evidence.dmlEvents.some(event => event.dml && event.table === "unknown")) throw new Error("required integration DML trace is not table-bound");
  if (evidence.dmlEvents.some(event => event.dml) && !evidence.projectionInvocation.calledBeforeFirstDml) throw new Error("required integration projection did not precede DML");
  const projectionRequired = new Set(["progress.error.invalid-projection-no-dml", "progress.atomic.positive-projection-conflict", "progress.atomic.valid-no-score"]);
  if (projectionRequired.has(context.id) && (evidence.projectionInvocation.projectorCalls !== 1 || evidence.projectionInvocation.validatorCalls !== 1 || evidence.projectionInvocation.realExtractorCalled !== true || evidence.projectionInvocation.realValidatorCalled !== true || evidence.projectionInvocation.sourceModuleId !== "apps/web/src/lib/leaderboard-extractors.ts" || evidence.projectionInvocation.projectorSequence === null || evidence.projectionInvocation.validatorSequence === null || !evidence.projectionInvocation.calledBeforeFirstDml)) throw new Error("required real projection and validator evidence is missing");
}

proofCases, REQUIRED_INTEGRATION_ORACLES, and REQUIRED_INTEGRATION_CASES are emitted by the same manifest compiler as the closure table and required-ID projection. The 21 requiredIntegrationSeeds contain function-valued setup, assertion, and evidence factories; the map and bound cases are generated projections, not a second hand-authored test inventory. proofCases includes every named closure case exactly once. testName is the literal test name passed to the test runner, equal to proofId; file is the normalized path of an actual test file collected by the gate. Type probes, DB-harness checks, and config checks are invoked inside their mapped real test, so no registry row points to a source, config, or helper file that cannot emit a terminal test event. assertProofCaseRegistry checks that every closure ID has exactly one binding, every binding has exactly one closure ID, every file is in the file inventory and is matched by the ordinary or integration include glob for its gate, and the file and test-name pair is unique. assertRequiredIntegrationCaseRegistry separately requires exact set equality and cardinality 21 between the generated required-ID projection and REQUIRED_INTEGRATION_CASES, exact per-ID oracle identity and brand, callable setup/assertion/evidence functions, and every binding in the narrow integration file. Every IntegrationEvidenceLedger method is implemented by the isolated route/DB harness, not by a flag setter: setup methods create the named fixture, capture a private setup receipt with real request IDs and pre-state snapshots, and release the real barrier only after both concurrent request observations are recorded; assertion methods read the actual response, lock event sequence, table snapshots, envelope digests, and DML trace and append a private assertion receipt. assertScenarioLedgerComplete returns a private completion object that retains both receipts, the observed route invocation IDs, and a digest, and rejects a missing brand, wrong case, wrong phase, mismatched request set, non-monotonic sequence, incomplete snapshot, or an assertion that did not observe the real route. terminalEvidence accepts that exact completion object, copies its digest, and refuses to return until those exact receipts exist and its private invocation token matches the oracle; discarding the completion return value cannot manufacture terminal evidence. Transactional cases require a non-empty transaction trace; preflight no-DML cases require an actual route response, empty transaction/projection traces, and no fabricated lock, envelope, or DML evidence. Calling a seed or assertion with no real route or DB observation throws. The changed ConcurrentFirstSaveSetup starts two real requests behind one barrier, records both as started, allows only the request whose observed first user-lock grant wins to observe the absent row, records the other request queued on the user lock, releases only after both waiters are observed, and records the actual winner/loser order; the assertion invokes its bindObservedRoles function with that observed order and rejects fixed winner labels. runRequiredIntegrationCase increments the context call ledger only around the actual function invocation, requires setup before assertions, requires assertions before terminal evidence, passes the same context to all three functions, and calls assertRequiredIntegrationInvocation before the reporter can accept the result. A proof case cannot be represented by a section label, a filename suffix, an aggregate assertion, or a function that was never called.

The first-save role check is receipt-bound: seedConcurrentFirstSave returns the private ConcurrentFirstSaveSetupReceipt only after the real lock observers record the actual first grant, absent-row observation, queued waiter, request IDs, and sequence. The ledger's assertion implementation calls getConcurrentFirstSaveSetup(), passes that receipt to assertObservedFirstSaveRoleBinding, and rejects when the function's winner or loser differs from the observed request mapping, when one request is missing, or when the assertion receipt does not carry the same role evidence. Static request labels in the setup contract only name the two possible requests; they never select the winner.

The GET lock oracle is route-derived and sequence-based. The route records the route invocation ID on the user-lock grant, the authoritative progress_owner_epochs FOR UPDATE select, the app-lock grant, and the app-row select-after-lock with transaction IDs and monotonic sequence numbers. deriveGetLockOrderEvidence accepts only that correlated non-empty event ledger, requires one transaction, exactly one app-lock request/grant/select sequence, and returns a private branded read proof. progress.lock.read-after-lock requires strict sequence order user grant < authority FOR UPDATE < app grant < app select, a response byte digest matching the committed row, zero app DML, and no authority-probe evidence. Recovery and claim probes call the same derivation with mode:"recover" or mode:"claim"; the authority-probe branch rejects every app-lock event and returns literal zero request, grant, select, and recovery counts. lineage.owner-epoch.test.ts runs both probe modes, checks the branded zero-app-lock traces, replays the exact claim and recovery nonce, and fails when a changed tuple reuses a nonce. The terminal gate rejects a caller-supplied summary or a GET proof that only says it waited or read after a lock without these observations.

The serialized proof channel is executable rather than descriptive. Each process emits a version-1 ProofRunSummary through serializeProofRunSummary; it carries one generated runId, the manifest digest, the runner-specific config digest, exact sorted expected/observed IDs, required IDs, run status, error count, and a digest of its private execution registry. parseProofRunSummary checks the exact wire keys, rejects malformed JSON, extra keys, duplicate or unsorted IDs, failed runs, missing fields, and nonzero unhandled errors, then rebrands only the validated object. createProofRunController is the two-process aggregator: it accepts the serialized channel string, requires one matching run ID, one manifest digest, the expected config digest for each runner, one ordinary summary, and one integration summary, rejects duplicate or cross-run submissions, invokes assertProofCaseRunnerCoverage once after both summaries arrive, and requires finalize() to fail when one summary or the coverage callback is missing. Its onBothRunnersComplete callback is wired directly to the exact coverage function with the generated configs, proof cases, required IDs, and file inventory.

The following PROOF_CASES, REQUIRED_INTEGRATION_ORACLES, and REQUIRED_INTEGRATION_CASES blocks are generated-output snapshots shown in the plan for exact review. The implementation stores their source seeds only in ProgressSyncManifestInput and emits these maps/arrays from one generateProgressSyncManifest(input) call; createProofCaseContracts(input) consumes input.proofCaseSeeds from that same manifest instance, so no free-standing seed declaration can diverge from the generated projection. A hand-edited generated projection or a contract map from another input instance fails the manifest check.

function assertProofCaseRegistry(
  closureIds: readonly string[],
  proofCases: readonly ProofCaseBinding[],
  fileInventory: readonly FileInventoryInput[]
): void {
  const closure = [...closureIds].sort();
  const registry = proofCases.map((caseBinding) => caseBinding.proofId).sort();
  const files = new Set(fileInventory.map((row) => row.path));
  const testNames = new Set<string>();
  const fileTests = new Set<string>();
  if (closure.length !== registry.length || closure.some((id, index) => id !== registry[index])) throw new Error("proof case bidirectional mismatch");
  if (new Set(registry).size !== registry.length) throw new Error("duplicate proof ID");
  for (const caseBinding of proofCases) {
    if (!files.has(caseBinding.file)) throw new Error("proof file is not inventoried");
    if (caseBinding.testName !== caseBinding.proofId || testNames.has(caseBinding.testName)) throw new Error("duplicate or non-literal proof test name");
    const fileTest = `${caseBinding.file}\u0000${caseBinding.testName}`;
    if (fileTests.has(fileTest)) throw new Error("duplicate proof file/test pair");
    testNames.add(caseBinding.testName);
    fileTests.add(fileTest);
  }
}
import type { Reporter as VitestReporter, SerializedError, TestCase, TestModule, TestRunEndReason } from "vitest/node";
type ProofRunner = "ordinary" | "integration";
declare const proofReporterBrand: unique symbol;
type ProgressProofReporter = {
  readonly [proofReporterBrand]: true;
  readonly runner: ProofRunner;
  readonly executionRegistry: ProofExecutionRegistry;
  requiredIntegrationIds(): readonly string[];
  expectedTerminalIds(): readonly string[];
  terminalIds(): readonly string[];
  recordTerminal(evidence: ProofCaseExecutionEvidence): void;
};
declare const proofRunSummaryBrand: unique symbol;
type ProofRunSummary = {
  readonly [proofRunSummaryBrand]: true;
  wireVersion: 1;
  runId: string;
  manifestDigest: string;
  configDigest: string;
  runner: ProofRunner;
  terminalIds: readonly string[];
  expectedTerminalIds: readonly string[];
  requiredIntegrationIds: readonly string[];
  executionRegistryDigest: string;
  reason: TestRunEndReason;
  unhandledErrorCount: number;
};
declare function digestProofExecutionRegistry(registry: ProofExecutionRegistry): string;
type ProofRunController = {
  runnerEnded(serializedSummary: string): void;
  finalize(): void;
};
function assertCanonicalProofIds(ids: readonly string[]): void {
  const sorted = [...ids].sort();
  if (sorted.length !== ids.length || new Set(ids).size !== ids.length || ids.some((id, index) => id !== sorted[index]) || ids.some(id => id.length === 0)) throw new Error("proof summary IDs are not canonical");
}
function serializeProofRunSummary(summary: ProofRunSummary): string {
  if (summary[proofRunSummaryBrand] !== true || summary.wireVersion !== 1 || summary.runId.length === 0 || summary.manifestDigest.length === 0 || summary.configDigest.length === 0 || summary.executionRegistryDigest.length === 0) throw new Error("proof summary cannot be serialized");
  assertCanonicalProofIds(summary.terminalIds);
  assertCanonicalProofIds(summary.expectedTerminalIds);
  assertCanonicalProofIds(summary.requiredIntegrationIds);
  return stableJson({ wireVersion: summary.wireVersion, runId: summary.runId, manifestDigest: summary.manifestDigest, configDigest: summary.configDigest, runner: summary.runner, terminalIds: summary.terminalIds, expectedTerminalIds: summary.expectedTerminalIds, requiredIntegrationIds: summary.requiredIntegrationIds, executionRegistryDigest: summary.executionRegistryDigest, reason: summary.reason, unhandledErrorCount: summary.unhandledErrorCount });
}
function parseProofRunSummary(serializedSummary: string): ProofRunSummary {
  let parsed: unknown;
  try { parsed = JSON.parse(serializedSummary); } catch { throw new Error("malformed proof summary"); }
  if (parsed === null || typeof parsed !== "object" || Array.isArray(parsed)) throw new Error("malformed proof summary");
  const raw = parsed as Record<string, unknown>;
  const keys = Object.keys(raw).sort().join(",");
  if (raw === null || typeof raw !== "object" || Array.isArray(raw) || keys !== "configDigest,executionRegistryDigest,expectedTerminalIds,manifestDigest,reason,requiredIntegrationIds,runId,runner,terminalIds,unhandledErrorCount,wireVersion" || raw.wireVersion !== 1 || (raw.runner !== "ordinary" && raw.runner !== "integration") || typeof raw.runId !== "string" || typeof raw.manifestDigest !== "string" || typeof raw.configDigest !== "string" || typeof raw.executionRegistryDigest !== "string" || !Array.isArray(raw.terminalIds) || !Array.isArray(raw.expectedTerminalIds) || !Array.isArray(raw.requiredIntegrationIds) || raw.terminalIds.some(id => typeof id !== "string") || raw.expectedTerminalIds.some(id => typeof id !== "string") || raw.requiredIntegrationIds.some(id => typeof id !== "string") || typeof raw.unhandledErrorCount !== "number" || raw.unhandledErrorCount !== 0 || raw.reason !== "passed") throw new Error("malformed, failed, or cross-run proof summary");
  const terminalIds = raw.terminalIds as string[];
  const expectedTerminalIds = raw.expectedTerminalIds as string[];
  const requiredIntegrationIds = raw.requiredIntegrationIds as string[];
  assertCanonicalProofIds(terminalIds);
  assertCanonicalProofIds(expectedTerminalIds);
  assertCanonicalProofIds(requiredIntegrationIds);
  return { [proofRunSummaryBrand]: true, wireVersion: 1, runId: raw.runId, manifestDigest: raw.manifestDigest, configDigest: raw.configDigest, runner: raw.runner, terminalIds, expectedTerminalIds, requiredIntegrationIds, executionRegistryDigest: raw.executionRegistryDigest, reason: "passed", unhandledErrorCount: 0 };
}
function createProofRunController(
  runId: string,
  manifestDigest: string,
  configDigests: Readonly<Record<ProofRunner, string>>,
  onBothRunnersComplete: (summaries: readonly ProofRunSummary[]) => void
): ProofRunController {
  const summaries = new Map<ProofRunner, ProofRunSummary>();
  let coverageCompleted = false;
  return {
    runnerEnded(serializedSummary) {
      const summary = parseProofRunSummary(serializedSummary);
      if (summary.runId !== runId || summary.manifestDigest !== manifestDigest || summary.configDigest !== configDigests[summary.runner] || summaries.has(summary.runner)) throw new Error("proof summary run identity or config binding mismatch");
      summaries.set(summary.runner, summary);
      if (summaries.size === 2) {
        if (coverageCompleted || !summaries.has("ordinary") || !summaries.has("integration")) throw new Error("proof summary aggregator completed more than once");
        onBothRunnersComplete([summaries.get("ordinary")!, summaries.get("integration")!]);
        coverageCompleted = true;
      }
    },
    finalize() {
      if (summaries.size !== 2 || !summaries.has("ordinary") || !summaries.has("integration") || !coverageCompleted) throw new Error("proof summary channel is missing a runner or coverage completion");
    }
  };
}
type ProgressProofVitestReporter = VitestReporter & ProgressProofReporter & {
  onTestCaseResult(testCase: TestCase): Promise<void>;
  onTestRunEnd(testModules: ReadonlyArray<TestModule>, unhandledErrors: ReadonlyArray<SerializedError>, reason: TestRunEndReason): Promise<void>;
};
type ProofRunnerConfig = {
  runner: ProofRunner;
  runId: string;
  manifestDigest: string;
  configDigest: string;
  includeGlob: string;
  excludeGlobs: readonly string[];
  expectedTerminalIds: readonly string[];
  requiredIntegrationIds: readonly string[];
};
function digestProofRunnerConfig(config: ProofRunnerConfig): string {
  return sha256(stableJson({ runner: config.runner, includeGlob: config.includeGlob, excludeGlobs: [...config.excludeGlobs].sort(), expectedTerminalIds: [...config.expectedTerminalIds].sort(), requiredIntegrationIds: [...config.requiredIntegrationIds].sort() }));
}
function createProofCaseContract(seed: ProofCaseSeed): ProofCaseContract {
  return {
    [proofCaseContractBrand]: true,
    binding: seed,
    setup: seed.setup,
    assertions: seed.assertions,
    terminalEvidence: seed.terminalEvidence,
    async run(context) {
      let setupCalls = 0;
      let assertionCalls = 0;
      let terminalEvidenceCalls = 0;
      context.actualTestInvocation();
      setupCalls += 1;
      await seed.setup(context);
      assertionCalls += 1;
      await seed.assertions(context);
      terminalEvidenceCalls += 1;
      const terminal = await seed.terminalEvidence(context);
      if (terminal[proofObservationBrand] !== true || terminal.proofId !== seed.proofId || terminal.terminalObservation.phase !== "terminal") throw new Error("proof terminal observation is unbranded or mismatched");
      const observations = context.observations.requireComplete(seed.proofId);
      if (setupCalls !== 1 || assertionCalls !== 1 || terminalEvidenceCalls !== 1) throw new Error("proof contract hook count mismatch");
      return { [proofObservationBrand]: true, proofId: seed.proofId, runner: context.runner, terminal: "passed", testInvoked: true, setupCalls: 1, assertionCalls: 1, terminalEvidenceCalls: 1, oracleInvocationToken: terminal.oracleInvocationToken, projectorInvocation: terminal.projectorInvocation, observations };
    }
  };
}
declare const PROGRESS_SYNC_MANIFEST_INPUT: ProgressSyncManifestInput;
const PROOF_CASE_SEEDS: readonly ProofCaseSeed[] = PROGRESS_SYNC_MANIFEST_INPUT.proofCaseSeeds;
function createProofCaseContracts(input: ProgressSyncManifestInput): Readonly<Record<string, ProofCaseContract>> {
  const contracts = Object.fromEntries(input.proofCaseSeeds.map(seed => [seed.proofId, createProofCaseContract(seed)]));
  if (Object.keys(contracts).length !== input.proofCaseSeeds.length) throw new Error("manifest proof contract projection has duplicate IDs");
  return contracts;
}
const PROOF_CASE_CONTRACTS: Readonly<Record<string, ProofCaseContract>> = createProofCaseContracts(PROGRESS_SYNC_MANIFEST_INPUT);
async function runProofCase(contract: ProofCaseContract, context: ProofCaseExecutionContext): Promise<ProofCaseExecutionEvidence> {
  if (contract[proofCaseContractBrand] !== true || contract.binding.proofId !== context.proofId) throw new Error("proof contract brand or ID mismatch");
  if (context.executionRegistry[proofExecutionRegistryBrand] !== true) throw new Error("proof execution registry brand missing");
  const evidence = await contract.run(context);
  context.executionRegistry.record(evidence);
  return evidence;
}
function createProgressProofReporter(config: { runner: ProofRunner; runId: string; manifestDigest: string; configDigest: string; expectedTerminalIds: readonly string[]; requiredIntegrationIds: readonly string[]; executionRegistry: ProofExecutionRegistry; controller: ProofRunController }): ProgressProofVitestReporter {
  const expected = [...new Set(config.expectedTerminalIds)].sort();
  const required = [...new Set(config.requiredIntegrationIds)].sort();
  const expectedSet = new Set(expected);
  const requiredSet = new Set(required);
  const observed = new Map<string, ProofCaseExecutionEvidence>();
  let runEnded = false;
  const reporter: ProgressProofVitestReporter = {
    [proofReporterBrand]: true,
    runner: config.runner,
    executionRegistry: config.executionRegistry,
    requiredIntegrationIds: () => [...required],
    expectedTerminalIds: () => [...expected],
    terminalIds: () => [...observed.keys()].sort(),
    recordTerminal(evidence) {
      if (evidence.terminal !== "passed" || evidence.runner !== config.runner || evidence.testInvoked !== true || !expectedSet.has(evidence.proofId)) throw new Error("proof terminal event is unknown, failed, uninvoked, or on the wrong runner");
      if (config.executionRegistry[proofExecutionRegistryBrand] !== true || config.executionRegistry.get(evidence.proofId) !== evidence) throw new Error("proof terminal evidence was not recorded by the executed contract");
      if (observed.has(evidence.proofId)) throw new Error("duplicate proof terminal event");
      if (requiredSet.has(evidence.proofId) && (evidence.oracleInvocationToken === null || evidence.projectorInvocation === null || evidence.projectorInvocation[projectionInvocationEvidenceBrand] !== true || (evidence.projectorInvocation.firstDmlSequence !== null && !evidence.projectorInvocation.calledBeforeFirstDml))) throw new Error("required proof invocation evidence missing");
      observed.set(evidence.proofId, evidence);
    },
    async onTestCaseResult(testCase) {
      const proofId = testCase.name;
      if (!expectedSet.has(proofId)) return;
      const result = testCase.result();
      if (result.state !== "passed") throw new Error(`proof ${proofId} did not pass: ${result.state}`);
      const execution = config.executionRegistry.get(proofId);
      if (execution === undefined) throw new Error(`proof ${proofId} had no executed contract`);
      reporter.recordTerminal(execution);
    },
    async onTestRunEnd(_testModules, unhandledErrors, reason) {
      if (runEnded) throw new Error("duplicate proof run-end event");
      runEnded = true;
      if (reason !== "passed" || unhandledErrors.length !== 0) throw new Error("proof run did not finish cleanly");
      const actual = reporter.terminalIds();
      if (actual.length !== expected.length || actual.some((id, index) => id !== expected[index])) throw new Error("proof terminal IDs are incomplete");
      config.controller.runnerEnded(serializeProofRunSummary({ [proofRunSummaryBrand]: true, wireVersion: 1, runId: config.runId, manifestDigest: config.manifestDigest, configDigest: config.configDigest, runner: config.runner, terminalIds: actual, expectedTerminalIds: expected, requiredIntegrationIds: required, executionRegistryDigest: digestProofExecutionRegistry(config.executionRegistry), reason, unhandledErrorCount: unhandledErrors.length }));
    }
  };
  return reporter;
}
function runnerForProofCase(caseBinding: ProofCaseBinding): ProofRunner {
  return caseBinding.file.startsWith("apps/web/src/integration/progress/") ? "integration" : "ordinary";
}
function expectedTerminalIdsForRunner(
  proofCases: readonly ProofCaseBinding[],
  requiredIntegrationIds: readonly string[],
  runner: ProofRunner
): readonly string[] {
  const proofIds = proofCases.filter((caseBinding) => runnerForProofCase(caseBinding) === runner).map((caseBinding) => caseBinding.proofId);
  const projectedIds = runner === "integration" ? [...proofIds, ...requiredIntegrationIds] : proofIds;
  return [...new Set(projectedIds)].sort();
}
function assertProofCaseRunnerCoverage(
  proofCases: readonly ProofCaseBinding[],
  requiredIntegrationIds: readonly string[],
  fileInventory: readonly FileInventoryInput[],
  configs: readonly ProofRunnerConfig[],
  runSummaries: readonly ProofRunSummary[]
): void {
  assertRequiredIntegrationCaseRegistry(requiredIntegrationIds, REQUIRED_INTEGRATION_CASES, fileInventory);
  if (configs.length !== 2 || runSummaries.length !== 2) throw new Error("proof runner requires exactly ordinary and integration runs");
  const runners = configs.map((config) => config.runner).sort();
  const summaryRunners = runSummaries.map(summary => summary.runner).sort();
  const runIds = new Set(configs.map(config => config.runId));
  const manifestDigests = new Set(configs.map(config => config.manifestDigest));
  if (runners.join(",") !== "integration,ordinary" || summaryRunners.join(",") !== "integration,ordinary" || new Set(runners).size !== 2 || new Set(summaryRunners).size !== 2 || runIds.size !== 1 || manifestDigests.size !== 1 || configs.some(config => config.runId.length === 0 || config.manifestDigest.length === 0 || config.configDigest !== digestProofRunnerConfig(config) || config.expectedTerminalIds.length === 0 || config.requiredIntegrationIds.some(id => !requiredIntegrationIds.includes(id))) || runSummaries.some(summary => summary[proofRunSummaryBrand] !== true || summary.wireVersion !== 1 || summary.reason !== "passed" || summary.unhandledErrorCount !== 0 || summary.executionRegistryDigest.length === 0 || summary.runId !== configs[0].runId || summary.manifestDigest !== configs[0].manifestDigest || summary.configDigest !== configs.find(config => config.runner === summary.runner)!.configDigest)) throw new Error("proof runner runner-set or serialized identity mismatch");
  const contractIds = Object.keys(PROOF_CASE_CONTRACTS).sort();
  const proofIds = proofCases.map(caseBinding => caseBinding.proofId).sort();
  if (contractIds.length !== proofIds.length || contractIds.some((id, index) => id !== proofIds[index]) || Object.values(PROOF_CASE_CONTRACTS).some(contract => contract[proofCaseContractBrand] !== true || typeof contract.run !== "function" || typeof contract.setup !== "function" || typeof contract.assertions !== "function" || typeof contract.terminalEvidence !== "function")) throw new Error("proof contract registry mismatch");
  for (const config of configs) {
    if (config.runner === "ordinary" && (config.includeGlob !== "src/**/*.{test,spec}.{js,jsx,ts,tsx}" || !config.excludeGlobs.includes("src/integration/**"))) throw new Error("ordinary proof glob mismatch");
    if (config.runner === "integration" && config.includeGlob !== "src/integration/progress/**/*.integration.test.ts") throw new Error("integration proof glob mismatch");
    const ids = expectedTerminalIdsForRunner(proofCases, requiredIntegrationIds, config.runner);
    const expected = [...config.expectedTerminalIds].sort();
    if (ids.length !== expected.length || ids.some((id, index) => id !== expected[index])) throw new Error("proof runner expected-ID mismatch");
    const reporterRequired = [...config.requiredIntegrationIds].sort();
    const expectedRequired = config.runner === "integration" ? [...requiredIntegrationIds].sort() : [];
    if (reporterRequired.length !== expectedRequired.length || reporterRequired.some((id, index) => id !== expectedRequired[index])) throw new Error("proof reporter required-ID mismatch");
    const summary = runSummaries.find(candidate => candidate.runner === config.runner);
    if (summary === undefined || summary.expectedTerminalIds.length !== ids.length || [...summary.expectedTerminalIds].sort().some((id, index) => id !== ids[index]) || summary.requiredIntegrationIds.length !== expectedRequired.length || [...summary.requiredIntegrationIds].sort().some((id, index) => id !== expectedRequired[index]) || [...summary.terminalIds].sort().some((id, index) => id !== ids[index])) throw new Error("serialized proof run summary mismatch");
  }
  const expectedUnion = [...new Set(configs.flatMap(config => config.expectedTerminalIds))].sort();
  const observedUnion = [...new Set(runSummaries.flatMap(summary => summary.terminalIds))].sort();
  if (expectedUnion.length !== observedUnion.length || expectedUnion.some((id, index) => id !== observedUnion[index])) throw new Error("proof run summary union mismatch");
}

Every generated proof case has one runner derived from its actual inventoried file and one function-valued ProofCaseContract in PROOF_CASE_CONTRACTS. Both Vitest configs pass the branded ProgressProofVitestReporter, which implements the installed Vitest 4 onTestCaseResult(testCase) and onTestRunEnd(testModules,unhandledErrors,reason) declarations; ordinary uses the exact ordinary include and must include src/integration/** in excludeGlobs, while integration uses the exact narrow integration include. The generator emits literal it(proofId, async () => runProofCase(PROOF_CASE_CONTRACTS[proofId],context)) cases in the mapped file. Required integration seeds first run runRequiredIntegrationCase, then pass its private branded IntegrationTerminalEvidence through createProofTerminalObservation; the bridge rejects a mismatched ID, missing integration brand, missing invocation token, or non-terminal observation, so a string-token adapter cannot manufacture proof evidence. runProofCase records its private branded evidence in one per-run ProofExecutionRegistry, and the reporter accepts evidence only when the exact object is present in that registry for the actual Vitest test name. Failed, skipped, todo, filtered, nonterminal, unhandled, duplicate, or never-invoked expected proof events fail before coverage can pass; unrelated ordinary test events are ignored by the proof reporter. Each contract must invoke setup, assertions, and terminal evidence exactly once and must return a private branded non-empty observation ledger; a function that only returns a token or is never invoked cannot produce execution evidence. Ordinary mode reports every client, policy, and ordinary-server proof ID exactly once. Integration mode reports the set union of every integration proof ID and the generated 21 required integration IDs exactly once; an ID present in both projections, including progress.atomic.valid-no-score, emits one terminal observation. The 21-ID set is a required subset with cardinality 21, not a replacement for named integration proof cases. A required reporter event is accepted only when its terminal state is passed, its proof ID and runner are registered, its contract evidence has the private brand and exact one-call counts, and any required integration case carries the private oracle invocation token plus branded projection invocation evidence. Reporter state derives observed IDs from real onTestCaseResult events and has no caller-supplied observed-ID field. Because pnpm test and pnpm test:integration are separate processes, each reporter keeps its own registry and emits one ProofRunSummary containing exact expected/observed IDs, required IDs, run status, error count, and a digest of its private execution registry. The existing proof controller receives the two summaries through the runner's serialized summary channel, rejects missing, duplicate, malformed, or cross-run summaries, and calls assertProofCaseRunnerCoverage exactly once after both summaries arrive; it checks each per-run set and the exact ordinary/integration union. A missing runner-end event or failed run leaves the controller incomplete. proof.runner-coverage supplies one valid ordinary config, one missing-exclusion config that must fail, the overlapping-ID case, a never-invoked contract, and duplicate/filtered/failed terminal events.

Fixed implementation boundary. The artifact is the only file edited in this pass. The files listed in Section 11 are the exact future implementation inventory. No claim below means that implementation code or tests have already changed.

2. Owner tuple, state machine, and first-sync truth table

2.1 One document-wide coordinator

The browser module exports exactly one handle named documentProgressSync. It is stored on the browser global under Symbol.for("hanks-hits.progress-sync.coordinator.v8") so duplicate module evaluation still returns the same handle. Server rendering never creates this browser coordinator. useAuthSync, AchievementCelebrations, HomeClient recovery, and the generated binding for each of the 32 stores use this handle. No component creates a coordinator, owner tuple, interval, marker reader, or transport queue.

type VirtualRegistrationSlot = {
  appId: ValidAppId;
  registrationOrder: number;
  status: "virtual" | "live";
  hydration: VirtualHydrationResult;
  storeApi: SynchronizedStoreApi<unknown, unknown> | null;
  refCount: number;
};
type VirtualHydrationResult =
  | { kind: "virtual-noop"; valid: true; hasHydrated: true; persisted: "absent"; canonicalDefault: true; storeApi: null }
  | { kind: "live"; valid: true; hasHydrated: true; persisted: "absent" | "valid"; canonicalDefault: boolean; storeApi: SynchronizedStoreApi<unknown, unknown> };

Coordinator initialization constructs exactly 32 VirtualRegistrationSlot records from the generated manifest in VALID_APP_IDS order, A1 through A32. A virtual slot owns a generated canonical-default descriptor and a valid VirtualHydrationResult with hasHydrated:true; it performs no storage read, persist write, converter adoption, network call, or mutation publication. The coordinator's READY predicate accepts only kind:"virtual-noop" or a validated live result, so an unmounted app cannot hold the document in perpetual loading. A real store constructor calls bindStore(appId,api) synchronously before any action, api.setState, persist write, or rehydrate; that call changes the preallocated slot to live, replaces the no-op result only after its guarded rehydrate resolves, records the API identity, and returns a lease. Binding the same API again is idempotent and increments only refCount; binding another API for the same app throws a configuration error. The slot order never changes when a route dynamically imports a store. A sign-out/reset visits the slots in A1-A32 order, resets only live APIs, records failed live app IDs, and leaves virtual slots with their valid no-op result; a later bind receives the already verified owner state and then hydrates. store-binding.virtual-32.test.ts asserts 32 valid no-op results before any route mount, zero storage/backend calls, and READY eligibility, while coordinator.achievements-and-chess.strictmode.test.tsx mounts achievements and Chess together and proves both bind preallocated slots under one generation.
