12.2 Acceptance matrix

IDAcceptance proof
A1All 32 source app IDs, inventory rows, runtime registrations, and generated route IDs are exact and unique.
A2One coordinator handle owns achievements plus a game under StrictMode; registration does not rotate generation.
A3All 32 real stores use construction-time owned PersistStorage and no default Zustand storage.
A4Invalid persisted bytes leave live state unchanged, keep hasHydrated false, and prevent network/READY.
A5Flat, wrapped, 2048 full-store, Chess, and achievements converters round-trip full/live/persisted/canonical projections; converter.full-store-2048 proves board/runtime preservation.
A6Non-persisting adoption and rollback preserve unrelated runtime state and publish no mutation.
A7Foreign purge verifies marker B before B1, every target read-back, quarantine, retries, recovery, and terminal marker behavior.
A8A real A write and A pagehide cannot clear B after B owns the marker.
A9Full attempt identity rejects late, stale, mismatched, and superseded responses.
A10GET server baseline and POST acknowledged baseline remain distinct through S1-in-flight reconcile.
A11One commitSourceMutation API covers all source actions, direct actions, same-byte mutations, throws, retries, replays, and async completion.
A12Guest first-login truth table transfers meaningful local data and never sends empty/default saves.
A13HomeClient and localProgress use centralized owner/key accessors, with device alias exclusion.
A14Chess canonical/legacy precedence, unknown bytes, write-back, read-back, rollback, and deletion order are exact.
A15GET, fetch POST, beacon POST, and DELETE builders/parsers match method-specific schemas and correlation.
A16Retry-After parsing uses fixed injected clock, date/delta precedence, malformed-header fallback, and bounded backoff.
A17ProgressTx and ProgressTxContext compile against installed Drizzle/pg declarations with no unsafe casts.
A18Progress/profile/leaderboard DML preserves IDs, timestamps, score rules, and same-transaction handle retries.
A19Isolated PostgreSQL imports env first, uses the production adapter, closes pools, and cleans up on failure.
A20Real locks map 55P03 and 57014 exactly; post-DML fault rolls back all row snapshots.
A21Route method schemas, raw validation order, errors, headers, auth/rate-limit collisions, and client compatibility are exact.
A22Ordinary Vitest excludes integration and the reporter rejects missing, duplicate, skipped, filtered, or unknown terminal observations.
A23SyncMetadataV1 has one local/wire/DB location, caps, legacy migration, reset comparison, and typed failure behavior.
A24Path grammar compiles, round-trips exact encodeURIComponent bytes, and rejects non-canonical forms.
A25All 32 policy rows are source-grounded; no default scalar policy remains.
A26MAX/MIN/BAL/SET/ID/AUTH/OPAQUE/RESET/tombstone/null/missing/equal-time semantics have divergent independent cases.
A27Math Attack totalCorrect and every other monotonic field fail under a timestamp-LWW regression.
A28Every partialized field has mutation provenance, including Drawing async thumbnail completion and achievement watermarks exclusion.
A29One authoritative input graph derives runtime, storage, policy, metadata, mutations, inventory, projections, and IDs.
A30Independent source/oracle projections have exact bidirectional equality and cardinality for apps, paths, actions, metadata, and cases.
A31All required implementation files, callers, stores, route/DB/harness modules, and tests are inventoried with consumers.
A32Strict HTML, code-block encoding, no em dash, whitespace, pre-edit fingerprint, and deterministic validation prove this artifact scope.

12.3 Deterministic validation commands

PLAN=docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-03-progress-sync-integrity.html

# Strict HTML structure, entities, and required counts. Execute the embedded no-file
# validator from Section 12.4 directly from this plan, without creating a helper file.
python3 - "$PLAN" <<'PY'
from html import unescape
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys
text = Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
marker = chr(60) + 'pre id="inline-plan-validator">' + chr(60) + 'code' + chr(62)
closing = chr(60) + '/code' + chr(62) + chr(60) + '/pre' + chr(62)
assert text.count(marker) == 1
start = text.index(marker)
end = text.index(closing, start)
body = text[start + len(marker):end]
exec(unescape(body), {"__name__": "__inline_plan_validator__"})
PY

# The validator also rejects the forbidden punctuation, ambiguity phrases, generic live port,
# generated timestamp fallback, unsafe transaction casts, raw fallback storage, old notification names,
# and incoming-state fallback. It checks every code block for escaped markup.

# Installed Zustand 5 declaration probe. This is an executable no-file compiler-host command.
# It intercepts one virtual source path while resolving every imported declaration from the
# installed workspace packages, and fails on any TypeScript diagnostic.
pnpm --dir apps/web exec tsx -e '
import ts from "typescript";
import path from "node:path";
const fileName = path.join(process.cwd(), "src/__virtual_zustand5_probe.ts");
const source = `
import type { StoreApi } from "zustand/vanilla";
import type { UseBoundStore } from "zustand/react";
import type { PersistOptions, PersistStorage, StateStorage, StorageValue } from "zustand/middleware";
type T = { count: number };
type P = { count: number };
declare const raw: StateStorage<void>;
declare const persistStorage: PersistStorage<P, void>;
declare const storageValue: StorageValue<P>;
const options: PersistOptions<T, P, void> = {
  name: "virtual-zustand5-probe",
  storage: persistStorage,
  partialize: (state) => ({ count: state.count }),
  version: 1,
  skipHydration: true,
  merge: (persisted, current) => current,
};
type ReadonlyApi<T> = Pick<StoreApi<T>, "getState" | "getInitialState" | "subscribe">;
type Hybrid<T, P> = UseBoundStore<ReadonlyApi<T>> & {
  getState(): T;
  setState(next: T): void;
  subscribe(listener: (state: T, previous: T) => void): () => void;
  persist: {
    rehydrate(): void | Promise<void>;
    hasHydrated(): boolean;
    getOptions(): Partial<PersistOptions<T, P, void>>;
  };
};
declare const api: Hybrid<T, P>;
const selected: number = api((state) => state.count);
const current: T = api();
const optionsFromRuntime: Partial<PersistOptions<T, P, void>> = api.persist.getOptions();
const rehydrated: void | Promise<void> = api.persist.rehydrate();
void raw; void storageValue; void options; void selected; void current; void optionsFromRuntime; void rehydrated;
`;
const options = {
  strict: true,
  target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022,
  module: ts.ModuleKind.NodeNext,
  moduleResolution: ts.ModuleResolutionKind.NodeNext,
  skipLibCheck: false,
  noEmit: true,
};
const host = ts.createCompilerHost(options);
const originalGetSourceFile = host.getSourceFile.bind(host);
host.getSourceFile = (name, languageVersion, onError, shouldCreateNewSourceFile) =>
  name === fileName
    ? ts.createSourceFile(name, source, languageVersion, true)
    : originalGetSourceFile(name, languageVersion, onError, shouldCreateNewSourceFile);
const program = ts.createProgram([fileName], options, host);
const diagnostics = ts.getPreEmitDiagnostics(program);
if (diagnostics.length) {
  console.error(ts.formatDiagnosticsWithColorAndContext(diagnostics, { getCanonicalFileName: x => x, getCurrentDirectory: () => process.cwd(), getNewLine: () => "\\n" }));
  process.exit(1);
}
console.log("installed Zustand 5 declaration probe: PASS");
'

# Installed Drizzle ORM and Node pg declaration probe. This uses the same virtual
# compiler-host technique and writes no source file. It verifies the exact schema
# namespace, pool client, relational query API, nested transaction callback, and
# derived ProgressTx shape required by the production adapter.
# It uses the db package's actual ESNext/Bundler settings and skipLibCheck=true;
# the workspace contains two transitive @types/node versions, so library-file
# duplicate diagnostics are suppressed while diagnostics in this virtual source
# still fail the command.
pnpm --dir packages/db exec tsx -e '
import ts from "typescript";
import path from "node:path";
const fileName = path.join(process.cwd(), "src/__virtual_progress_db_probe.ts");
const source = `
import * as schema from "@hank-neil/db/schema";
import type { NodePgDatabase } from "drizzle-orm/node-postgres";
import type { Pool, PoolClient } from "pg";
type ProgressDb = NodePgDatabase<typeof schema> & { $client: Pool };
type ProgressTx = Parameters<Parameters<ProgressDb["transaction"]>[0]>[0];
declare const db: ProgressDb;
declare const tx: ProgressTx;
const progressQuery = tx.query.appProgress.findFirst;
const profileQuery = tx.query.gamingProfiles.findFirst;
const transactionQuery = tx.query.appTransactions.findFirst;
const nested = tx.transaction(async (inner) => {
  await inner.query.leaderboardEntries.findFirst();
});
const pool: Pool = db.$client;
const clientPromise: Promise<PoolClient> = pool.connect();
void progressQuery; void profileQuery; void transactionQuery; void nested; void pool; void clientPromise;
`;
const options = {
  strict: true,
  target: ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022,
  module: ts.ModuleKind.ESNext,
  moduleResolution: ts.ModuleResolutionKind.Bundler,
  skipLibCheck: true,
  noEmit: true,
};
const host = ts.createCompilerHost(options);
const originalGetSourceFile = host.getSourceFile.bind(host);
host.getSourceFile = (name, languageVersion, onError, shouldCreateNewSourceFile) =>
  name === fileName
    ? ts.createSourceFile(name, source, languageVersion, true)
    : originalGetSourceFile(name, languageVersion, onError, shouldCreateNewSourceFile);
const program = ts.createProgram([fileName], options, host);
const diagnostics = ts.getPreEmitDiagnostics(program);
if (diagnostics.length) {
  console.error(ts.formatDiagnosticsWithColorAndContext(diagnostics, { getCanonicalFileName: x => x, getCurrentDirectory: () => process.cwd(), getNewLine: () => "\\n" }));
  process.exit(1);
}
console.log("installed Drizzle/pg declaration probe: PASS");
'

The HTML validator and Zustand declaration probe are no-file commands: the former is the inline source in Section 12.4 and the latter is a virtual compiler-host source described in Section 3. No validator helper file is part of the implementation inventory. During this amendment, equivalent read-only validation is run inline without creating a validator file. The tracked pre-edit fingerprint above was captured before this pass and must match exactly; the empty tracked diff and status allowlist fail on any product, test, config, lockfile, lesson, instruction, or non-preexisting untracked path. The aggregate hash over untracked graphify artifacts is diagnostic only because those artifacts predate this pass and are explicitly outside the edit scope.

12.4 Inline validator required by this pass

from html.parser import HTMLParser
from pathlib import Path
import re

plan = Path("docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-03-progress-sync-integrity.html")
text = plan.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
assert "\u2014" not in text
for phrase in ("cho" + "ose", "eith" + "er", "as " + "needed", "for " + "example", "option" + "al", "place" + "holder"):
    assert not re.search(r"\b" + re.escape(phrase) + r"\b", text, re.I)
for forbidden in ("LiveState" + "Port", "SCALAR" + "_LWW", "tx as " + "unknown", "return " + "incoming", "default " + "storage"):
    assert forbidden not in text
for stale in ("observed" + "TerminalIds", "observed" + "Ids", "setup: " + "readonly string[]", "assertions: " + "readonly string[]", "typed-409" + "-or-503-mapping", "client " + "verifies"):
    assert stale not in text

class Strict(HTMLParser):
    void = {"area", "base", "br", "col", "embed", "hr", "img", "input", "link", "meta", "param", "source", "track", "wbr"}
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(convert_charrefs=False)
        self.stack = []
        self.code_depth = 0
        self.code_raw_markup = []
    def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
        if self.code_depth and tag not in {"code", "pre"}:
            self.code_raw_markup.append(tag)
        if tag == "code":
            self.code_depth += 1
        if tag not in self.void:
            self.stack.append(tag)
    def handle_startendtag(self, tag, attrs):
        assert tag in self.void
    def handle_endtag(self, tag):
        assert self.stack and self.stack[-1] == tag, (tag, self.stack[-5:])
        self.stack.pop()
        if tag == "code":
            self.code_depth -= 1
    def handle_entityref(self, name):
        pass
    def handle_charref(self, name):
        pass
    def error(self, message):
        raise AssertionError(message)

parser = Strict()
parser.feed(text)
parser.close()
assert not parser.stack
assert not parser.code_raw_markup

inventory = re.search(r'<table id="sync-inventory".*?</table>', text, re.S).group(0)
inventory_ids = re.findall(r'data-inventory-id="(A\d+)"', inventory)
assert inventory_ids == [f"A{i}" for i in range(1, 33)]
matrix = re.search(r'<table id="semantic-matrix".*?</table>', text, re.S).group(0)
assert re.findall(r'data-matrix-id="(A\d+)"', matrix) == inventory_ids
acceptance = re.search(r'<table id="acceptance".*?</table>', text, re.S).group(0)
acceptance_ids = re.findall(r'<td>A(\d+)</td>', acceptance)
assert sorted(map(int, acceptance_ids)) == list(range(1, 33))
file_inventory = re.search(r'<table id="file-inventory".*?</table>', text, re.S).group(0)
file_paths = re.findall(r'<tr><td><code>(.*?)</code></td>', file_inventory)
assert len(file_paths) == 162 and len(set(file_paths)) == 162
assert all((path.startswith("apps/web/") or path.startswith("packages/db/")) and ".." not in path and "//" not in path for path in file_paths)
closure_table = re.search(r'<table id="round-seven-closure".*?</table>', text, re.S).group(0)
closure_thirds = re.findall(r'<tr><td>.*?</td><td>.*?</td><td>(.*?)</td></tr>', closure_table, re.S)
closure_ids = re.findall(r'<code>([^<]+)</code>', "".join(closure_thirds))
round_nine = re.search(r'<table id="round-nine-blocker-closure".*?</table>', text, re.S).group(0)
assert re.findall(r'<tr><td>(\d+)</td>', round_nine) == [str(i) for i in range(1, 28)]
assert all(label in round_nine for label in ("S1", "S2", "S3", "S4", "S5", "S6", "S7", "S8"))
capacity_table = re.search(r'<table id="metadata-capacity".*?</table>', text, re.S).group(0)
assert capacity_table.count("32768") == 1 and capacity_table.count("4194304") == 1 and "5000" in capacity_table and "nestedScopeDepth 3" in capacity_table
proof_registry = re.search(r'const PROOF_CASES:.*?\];', text, re.S).group(0)
registry_ids = re.findall(r'proof' + r'Id: "([^"]+)"', proof_registry)
assert len(closure_ids) == len(registry_ids) and sorted(closure_ids) == sorted(registry_ids)
assert len(set(registry_ids)) == len(registry_ids)
registry_files = re.findall(r'file' + r': "([^"]+)"', proof_registry)
registry_names = re.findall(r'test' + r'Name: "([^"]+)"', proof_registry)
assert len(registry_files) == len(registry_ids) == len(registry_names)
assert len(set(registry_names)) == len(registry_names)
assert all(path in file_paths for path in registry_files)
required = re.search(r'<pre id="required-test-ids"><code>(.*?)</code></pre>', text, re.S).group(1).split()
assert len(required) == 21 and len(set(required)) == 21
required_registry = re.search(r'const REQUIRED_INTEGRATION_CASES:.*?\];', text, re.S).group(0)
required_registry_ids = re.findall(r'\bid: "([^"]+)"', required_registry)
required_registry_files = re.findall(r'\bfile: "([^"]+)"', required_registry)
required_registry_names = re.findall(r'\btestName: "([^"]+)"', required_registry)
assert sorted(required_registry_ids) == sorted(required) and len(required_registry_ids) == 21 and len(set(required_registry_ids)) == 21
assert required_registry_names == required_registry_ids
assert required_registry_files == ["apps/web/src/integration/progress/progress.route.integration.test.ts"] * 21
required_oracles = re.search(r'const REQUIRED_INTEGRATION_ORACLES:.*?};', text, re.S).group(0)
assert required_oracles.count("createRequiredIntegrationOracle(") == 21
assert required_oracles.count("async context =&gt;") == 42
assert "context.calls.setup += 1" in text and "context.calls.assertions += 1" in text and "context.calls.terminalEvidence += 1" in text
assert "requiredIntegrationOracleBrand] !== true" in text

assert "createProofTerminalObservation" in text
assert required_oracles.count("scenario: ") == 40
assert matrix.count("data-matrix-id=") == 32
for token in ("StateStorage", "PersistStorage", "UseBoundStore", "skipHydration: true", "getOptions().storage", "OwnerTuple", "OwnerEpochLeaseV1", "OwnerEpochAuthorityRow", "OwnerLeaseSigner", 12.5 Fixed risks and unresolved status

RiskFixed mitigation
Old tabs retain stale memory.Every write, remove, transport, adoption, and pagehide action checks the full tuple. Stale bindings freeze and cannot clear a newer owner.
Legacy bytes have no trustworthy schema.Only named converters run. Unknown bytes quarantine and block; no invented schema is assigned.
Cross-tab storage operations fail or silently do nothing.Every marker and target operation has bounded retry and read-back. Terminal quarantine retains the marker and blocks READY.
GET baseline is mistaken for POST acknowledgement.Baseline kind is part of the lineage state and tests assert separate records.
Absolute totals are accidentally timestamp-LWW.All 32 matrix rows are generated from source paths and independent divergence tests include Math Attack totalCorrect.
Integration binds the production singleton.Env is set before dynamic imports, the factory takes the isolated handle, and pool identity is asserted.
Generated projections drift.One input graph plus independent source/oracle bidirectional equality and exact cardinality are release gates.
No unresolved blocker. The plan now has fixed owner, storage, hydration, purge, guest, lineage, wire, DB, isolation, metadata, policy, provenance, source-graph, file-inventory, test-ID, and verification contracts. The only remaining work is the implementation and its execution against the repository gates. This amendment makes no claim that those implementation files or tests have changed.

Generated from the jacked HTML plan template. This artifact is the implementation and acceptance contract for the progress-sync integrity repair.
